ParlaMint-AT_2012-12-06-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00185_d8e8750	Mr President. Thank you, Mrs. Minister! High house! The fact is that, despite the criticism of the Court of Auditors, which has already been discussed in detail, BIFIE contributes significantly to the implementation of the school reform. It has been very much involved in modernising and reforming our school system since 2008. The implementation of major reforms requires more transparent and, of course, efficient structures. An important step is also being taken with this BIFIE legislation. This includes appropriate funding for another three years and the extension of the advisory and information function of the Scientific Advisory Council and its members. The BIFIE also focuses on education standards and central matura, which are prepared by the Institute and implemented in parallel with and with schools. The standardized competence-oriented maturity test will create an Austria-wide comparable level of education that will provide a good foundation for each child's education and personality. The review, evaluation and feedback by the education standards ensure the development in improving the quality of teaching at each school location and also assist the schools in ensuring that teaching and learning outcomes are at the same time successful. When comparing the various school systems, it becomes clear that for a short or long time the common and full-day school must become a fixed part of our education system. In countries where these forms of school have already been realized, pupils are better off at different levels. Without this selection from ten years of age, the children also have a real chance of a truly comprehensive education. I mean, we need a school that brings a higher number of compulsory and higher school degrees, significantly lowers the number of school dropouts, promotes integration, leads to fewer youth unemployment and opens up more opportunities for all children. The educational offensive with many measures by Federal Minister Claudia Schmied has already shown a lot of positive results, and we will continue the reform and innovation spirit with many dedicated teachers, because finally the prosperity of our country will be decided in the classrooms.

Italy has recognised this, expressed its position on this point in Article 4 of its Constitution in 2004 and has incorporated these provisions. On this occasion, however, I would also like to thank all the employees who really have a bearing role here, especially my employee, Dr. Pointner, who is particularly supportive of me. Everyone does excellent work, all clubs really have absolute professionals at work, who work together very solution-oriented. [[Consistency with the SPÖ and the Abg. May. Musiol.]] We are now taking the next step, namely the organisational right of the Federal Administrative Court, and we are building on an organisational structure that already exists, namely the organisational structure of asylum rights. We only offer the opportunity to expand something. We take over that chamber system which is the asylum court has already given it, but it does not want to do so in a country-specific way, as it is organized in the asylum court, but rather to expand matter-specific. It is given the opportunity to take decisions in individual judges and Senate decisions – in tri-states or five-person states – and I believe that this system is a system capable of dealing with the attack that is expected to take up to 33,000 procedures. In addition, I believe that the President of the Asylum Court is a guarantee that he can manage and establish new courts, and that he is able to handle such a task in an organisational manner. I am sure that he will also succeed in implementing this major project of the Republic accordingly. This structure, which we are giving to the Federal Administrative Court here, is a very flexible structure, a structure which in the organization can give itself the Rules of Procedure by various committees, but also has the potential flexibility to produce and organize the Chambers accordingly, depending on how the attack is. Of course, this will only be possible in the course of practical implementation. We have an ISO-certified management structure there, so that the judges can rely on the second level to treat the file in the same way as it must be in line with a correct course of the administrative procedure, and can play its own free for exclusively judicial activity. However, the success or failure of this court will depend solely on the quality of the judges employed there. I am there a sense with all my colleagues in the Constitutional Committee that there must be quality, just like a judge. Therefore, it will be a very special task to look at people's qualifications when applying for the new judge's staff. However, I firmly believe that this next step is a positive step and a necessary step towards implementation. We already have the next meeting on procedural law tomorrow, and if we comply with this ambitious timetable, then, I believe, there is also enough time to adapt the more than a hundred legislation on matter by 2014, in order to allow a smooth transition into a federal administration court.

[[14:36:22]] 13. Point Report by the Constitutional Committee on Amendment No 2130/A by Mr Mag. Barbara Prammer, Fritz Neugebauer, colleagues concerning a federal law amending the compensation fund law and the Federal Law on the National Fund of the Republic of Austria for victims of National Socialism (2063 d.b.)
ParlaMint-AT_2012-12-06-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00185_d8e13829	We're coming to the 13th. Agenda item. There was no oral reporting. Mrs Mag. Mag. has spoken. Mulder. 3 minutes speaking time. - Please.
ParlaMint-AT_2012-12-06-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00185_d8e13856	Mr President. Mr State Secretary! Ladies and gentlemen! With this innovation, the General Compensation Fund is quickly and sparingly terminated with the application committee and arbitration body. Of course, there have been intensive discussions with the Federal Chancellery, the Ministry of Finance, the Ministry of Foreign Affairs, the National Council and the Fund. With the National Fund's Works Council, a staff plan has been drawn up for the reduction of employees. We therefore have a balanced, broad consensus-based initiative proposal, which includes the final steps of the General Compensation Fund and the gradual reduction of personnel. The Fund's task is to grant benefits to persons who have suffered losses or losses as a result of or in connection with events in the field of today's Austria in the period of National Socialism. For this purpose, the fund was equipped with 210 million US dollars. 20 702 applications for compensation have been received from the applicant committee. About 1 990 applications are still being processed. More than 1,000 applications concern finding heirs from applicants, namely worldwide, as these people live in over 60 countries. 40 million dollars are still available for distribution or disbursement. The arbitration body, which is responsible for natural refunds, has processed a total of 2,251 applications received. This amendment aims at the work of the General Fund for compensation by 31. ==References====External links== In order to achieve this objective, the applicant committee and also the arbitration body must submit a final report to the main committee, the applicant committee until 1. ==References====External links== September 2018. With the attention of the main committee, the two committees are then deemed to be dissolved. Another essential aspect of the novel is the reduction of the limitation period from 30 to five years. For claims already incurred, the limitation period begins with the entry into force of the legislative change. Thus, overall, it is ensured that, as early as possible, any remaining resources will be available for programmes in favour of the victims of National Socialism. I am pleased that this initiative has received the support of all the political groups in the Constitutional Committee, and I am convinced that this amendment brings the work of the General Fund to an orderly end as well as the use of remaining resources in good time for programmes. can take care of, in particular, socially in need of, the survivors of the Holocaust. The National Fund continues to exist, and I would like to thank all the employees for their work and for their commitment.

I deal exclusively with the § 312a of the Criminal Code decided on the occasion of the Novelle Service Law in the Committee and may show the critical consequences of this provision not sufficiently analysed from my point of view and consideration. It is, as it were, a new version of the Criminal Code, which is practically annexed to the law of the service, with the pre-criticism that the judicial committee's objective consistency would have been the right place – but please. The history of this new section consists in a supposed force for implementation of the UN Convention on Fundamental Rights, which, as I have already said in the Committee, originates from the legal and legal culture-based level of the Anglo-American legal system and which is not at all compatible with the continental European and, at times, Austrian origins and ideas. From the point of view of a lawyer and an occasional criminal defender, I will look forward to the reality of the provision with a certain pleasure. Who, then, is the object that is in question as a perpetrator, including, among other things, an official? – “Whoever () is a person, in particular in order to obtain a statement or confession from him or a third party, in order to punish them for an act actually or probably committed by her or a third party, in order to intimidate or require them or a third party, or for a reason of discrimination () cause great pain ()” – I shorten that. The second sentence could be accepted without any problem, but I would point out that, from tomorrow on, an official – whether with an administrative authority or with a court – who indicates to a suspected perpetrator that, in the absence of confession, a higher penalty is to be expected, in any case objectively realized the crime and clearly also the subjective crime side is associated with it, therefore the fulfillment of the crime and the crime under § 312a StGB can be expected. I also pointed out in the Committee that it must be considered in terms of content and subject matter whether, in criminal law, one would not have removed the grounds for compulsion – the most important cause of relief – of the so-called remorse confession as a command of self-punishment. The fact that this is a very massive rejection of the Austrian legal system is mentioned on the margins. Apart from the arguments that Mr Herbert will still present, this will result in a very massive uncertainty of the entire police staff, which from tomorrow will have to fear daily to make an agreement, because every half-way “weeded delinquent” – I say now, of course, under quotation marks – will be able to quickly extend this section and have prepared the pre-formed leaves on criminal charges against a policeman who tried to intimidate him because he wanted to lead him to a confession of a suspected act. [[Admissibility at the FPÖ]] [[Abg. Mayerhofer: Kreuzerl-Anzeige!]] So this provision, which clearly represents a translation of the content of the UN Convention on Fundamental Rights, without transforming it into Austrian law in an appropriate way – and to celebrate it as a high asset of a human rights renewal – is a fundamental wrong way. I am prophesying a considerable amount of atrocity that will result from the implementation of this provision. Enjoy all the officials! - Thank you.

That means you would have an anonymous application It would have been allowed to apply if it had not been approved at all. This is one of the 22 examples from 2010, in which the Federal Equal Treatment Commission found that discrimination has taken place. I cannot therefore understand why you do not support this request when it comes to your areas. Thank you.

At the latest there, Mrs. Winter, the employer or the employer would see if this is a woman, if it's someone who's disabled, if it's someone who's young, who's old and so on. No one here speaks of keeping the identity and face of the applicant or candidate secret until the day of appointment. You know, exactly such things, where you make fun of concrete suggestions to be able to reject them at any price, and then come with this whole cast of sugar: Yes, the evil, evil feminism!, and as if the equal treatment between men and women in our society was really achieved, so one makes himself ridiculous! We had already had a major attempt in Austria at the impact of collecting tuition fees, and at that time we could see that the study fees not only impact on the long-term students who do not really study at all – submission! – to discontinue their studies, but at the time there was a real significant fall of 15 percent in the number of initial students. It is therefore a very important step, from our point of view, that this law is accordingly repaired, that in future only those long-term students, who are no reason for a particularly long study period, such as professional activity or care obligations, and third-country nationals will be able to pay tuition fees. In this law, we have now introduced a differentiation among third-country nationals, according to which third-country nationals pay only if they come to Austria with the residence permit of study. Migrants who grow up in Austria, whose parents also contribute to our education system through their tax performance, are well aware that it is important to integrate them through education, which is equivalent to Austrians. We have long considered how we find a clean solution to clean this up, with two points important to me. The one point is – you have mentioned – that it must, of course, be constitutional. We have really discussed with top constitutional experts, and this is a admittedly surprising way for a non-Juristine, but one that is constitutional. The second point, which was also very important to me, is that we have found a scheme that does not lead to a call for universities to be able to collect autonomous tuition fees in the future. This certainly does not mean the retroactive renovation of this phase. Thirdly, there remains, of course, a certain injustice with the universities that adhered to the law during the phase and did not collect any tuition fees. We have agreed – the Minister will confirm this and has already confirmed it in the Committee – to find a financial compensation for those universities that have not cancelled tuition fees. As regards improving the funding of studies, it is important for me to stress that this is about increasing the amount of allowances for workers. This is important because, in the case of the great injustice in the system of study funding, we are starting to get through the different incomes concept. We also agree that this is a first important step, but it should not remain. We both see that there has to be a bigger throw.

ParlaMint-AT_2012-12-06-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00185_d8e20624	Mr President. Ladies and gentlemen, ladies and gentlemen. When the Constitutional Court repealed the 2008 Law, I immediately got to work and prepared a new bill. It has not succeeded in bringing these into line with the coalition partner, so that for a time a law was in force that left some gaps open. These gaps could be interpreted differently. I interpreted it with the probably best expert in the university law and on the basis of an opinion from him as the Constitutional Court probably did not interpret it – at least it indicated a statement from its chairman. With this statement he has linked the appeal to the policy to act here and to create legal certainty. I took this appeal seriously. I have entered into new negotiations with the coalition partner, and we have succeeded in establishing legal certainty in a, I believe, viable compromise. We have taken into account the appeal of the President of the Constitutional Court, and we have taken into account the desire of the universities for legal certainty. The result is that, from the summer semester, next year, all universities will once again remove student contributions for certain groups of persons: for long-term students, for extraordinary students, for students from third countries. In the case of third-country students, not all, but those where this is reasonable, the study contribution is doubled. This brings the universities safe additional income of approximately €40 million to €50 million a year. We have linked this measure to an increase in the funding of studies, and above all we are looking to see that the basis of assessment is increasing, which means that more students benefit from higher student funding. All of these are steps in the right direction. All this, and I know that, is not enough. I still feel that I am a follower of socially just study fees on a broader basis. Study fees, and these are my three arguments, are socially fair because they require a contribution from those who can afford to do so. This is especially true in Austria, where we have many international students who are also welcome, because it is not to be seen that the Austrian taxpayer makes the study places available to these students free of charge. [[Congratulations on the ÖVP.]] Study fees are also socially fair, because, as already mentioned, many other training courses – for example, master exams – are often very expensive and are not to be seen why they study one free of charge and the others finance their training with very high amounts. They are also socially fair because it has been shown that study contributions do not stop students from studying. The latest study is a 2011 study, made in Berlin with 40,000 cases that prove exactly that. I myself am a worker's child, I myself, when I started studying, paid for study contributions; these would not have stopped me from studying for a second. I know from my own experience that significant contributions do not. [[Intrusion of the Abg. Oils.]] That is why I still believe that there should be more study contributions. However, I also believe that the funding of studies, the study aid system should be extended accordingly. There are two working groups to this end. A working group set up the SPÖ after its party meeting. I am excited about the results of this working group. Another working group has set up the University Conference at my request, where we want to discuss and deliver results on this issue of good social security for students. If these results are available, I think we have a solid basis for further initiatives. I am glad that we reach this agreement because it brings legal certainty on this important issue and secures a certain sum for universities. I would like to thank all those who agree to this proposal.

If that's what we're going to do up to the 1st. Make January 2013, then we're still in time. I know that, so far, the other member states have the same time schedules as we have. So we haven't gone down yet. The advantage of this adjustment is to harmonise animal testing laws within the European Union. This is favourable for all interested parties, because it creates a certain level of equality. This benefits, on the one hand, the animals and, therefore, the concerns of the animal protectionists, because it is not possible to do so in countries with lower standards – at least not within the European Union, where we can regulate this. On the other hand, this adaptation creates similar framework conditions for research and the pharmaceutical industry. So the whole thing is good. We have tried to take into account both the interests of the animal protectionists and the legitimate interests of the research and pharmaceuticals industry, which, after all, creates many jobs in Austria. That was a difficult undertaking. Above all, of course, the animal protectionists have seen the possibility of bringing their concerns, which I consider to be very legitimate, into force as much as possible, namely the concern of making animal experiments almost impossible or prohibiting them in order to prevent animal suffering. Where the directive requires stricter measures, we are becoming more stringent. That is, we have done the best we can to take into account the interests of animal welfare. That is, I believe, a very important area that we must continue to promote and demand because it helps us to fight disease better. I think we have found a really good compromise. So we do a lot of things to make a good compromise here. That was a very strenuous work, I can say, very time-consuming, even emotionally very strenuous. Of course, I will also be grateful if we manage to implement this directive. Then we're still in time. I think we have a good base, taking into account the diverse and very divergent interests as well as well.

ParlaMint-AT_2012-12-06-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00185_d8e23257	Thank you, Minister of State! Ladies and gentlemen! With today's government proposal, we are continuing where we have already started yesterday to improve important social projects. Today we will close another gap with this government template. We will introduce a sickness allowance for the so-called “small” – under quotation marks – self-employed and entrepreneurs. This is planned in particular for those persons who either do not have employees or employ less than 25 employees. ParlaMint-AT_2012-12-06-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00185_d8e26060	Madam President. Minister! Ladies and gentlemen! The BZÖ's motion for a resolution on the Animal Health Act aims at a regulation to improve the living conditions of live horses. ParlaMint-AT_2012-12-06-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00185_d8e26088	Next speaker: Mr. Dr. Pirklhuber. 2 minutes remaining speaking time. - Please.
ParlaMint-AT_2012-12-06-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00185_d8e26109	Madam President. Federal Minister! Ladies and gentlemen! Very soon: In order to implement these two European framework laws or regulations, it should only be said that there is also a regulatory empowerment in the Animal Materials Act, and that is perhaps quite interesting, for biological-dynamic preparations. I hope the Minister will not use this regulatory authority. For those who may have no idea what that is: A biological-dynamic preparation, for example, is the horn-mist preparation. Take a cow's horn, fill it with manure, bury it in the earth – now my colleague Green Forest is already running its forehead – and after half a year this stuffed cowhorn is taken away and used as a biological-dynamic preparation in the fields. They will wonder: There are scientific evidence and studies published in “Nature” that such preparations have positive effects on soil fertility. So you also have such a regulatory authority with this law, Federal Minister. In addition to guidelines for the protection of animals in the killing, advice on the subject and control of slaughterhouses, EU legislation on animal welfare also deals with the rights of the ombudsman. In this context, I would like to make a plea for decentralized citizens. Add to the slaughter and for the slaughter of farmers, namely: The less animal transport, the more animal-friendly it will be during slaughter. The killing on the pasture, Mr. Federal Minister, is already permitted in Germany for special animal cavities, and this would also be an important instrument for pastures in Austria, especially in the case of all-year outdoor farming; we should work on this together perhaps. [[Intrusion of the Abg. Hörl]] - Thank you.
ParlaMint-AT_2012-12-06-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00185_d8e26154	Next speaker: Mrs Hellerer. - Please.
ParlaMint-AT_2012-12-06-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00185_d8e26176	Madam President. Federal Minister! I can also keep this very short from my side: The amendments to the Animal Materials Act – which has already been mentioned – are necessary because the rules on animal by-products not intended for human consumption have been revised at EU level and must therefore also be amended or adapted to national provisions. Of course, the EU regulation on the protection of animals at the time of the killing, which is implemented in its own legislative matter, must also be implemented by Austria. On the project, which Mr Pirklhuber mentioned, concerning the killing on pasture: This is also considered very reasonable by our side, because the catch of animals that live in the open country throughout the year is extremely difficult. It is associated with great stress for the animal, including for humans. There is a risk of accident, there are also accidents again and again, and an animal under stress also delivers a lower quality meat – meat quality, apologies! [[Call:No, quality]] In Germany, there is already the possibility of killing on the pasture, which is based on a derogation. I think that this would also be implemented in Austria. At least a pilot project could be started here, so that you can see how it really works in practice; you could build on it. On the application concerning the breeding horses: Fiaker companies exist in Vienna and Salzburg, and the rules of practice are governed by national law. It has also been mentioned that compliance with animal welfare rules is controlled and reviewed by the competent official veterinarians. The fact that the fictitious horses are kept excellent, I think you can convince yourself. I have also seen in Vienna with the teams that it is very well-kept horse riding that is on the way here, and I think that this picture of the Fiaker horses from the city view of Vienna and also from Salzburg would not be thought away, so we will not agree to this request either.

I'd like to go ahead, we're not in any Way against the Fiakers, quite the opposite: We stand with the Fiakers as part of the tradition. What we criticize is that there is no professional horse in the animal rearing regulation. There is the Animal Conservation Regulation for farmed animals, for favorite animals, for exodus, for animals living in unusual conditions, such as in animal homes, circuses, zoos and varietés. There is an animal welfare regulation for strenuous animals such as police and federal deer dogs, but not the professional Fiaker horse. It may be that the official veterinarians control, but according to what guidelines? You have no legal certainty. With the anchoring in the animal husbandry regulation, the official veterinarians would be helped because they have guidelines, the owners of fictitious horses, because they know what they need to stick to, and also the animal protectionists, because then we would have guidelines that regulate the accommodation and the attitude of the fictitious horses. It's not about anything else. I cannot understand why the government parties are making a travail. Maybe Consider You still do so and agree with the request. Thank you.
